-- SolAuth protected build dcadb725e8401ff3a9e52fc0
-- The security header below is a thin gate; the payload is your Luraph'd source.
return (function()
  local bit = bit32 or bit
  local env = (function()
-- SolAuth protected-build environment capture (pure Lua / Luau-safe).
--
-- Runs FIRST in the assembled header, before any of our own logic (and ideally
-- before an attacker can install fresh hooks in response to seeing us load). It
-- pulls the critical builtins straight off the base-thread global table via
-- rawget -- dodging __index proxies -- and freezes them into an `env` table that
-- every other module (guards, hwid, handshake) consumes. Because the rest of the
-- code calls THROUGH these captured references instead of the live globals, a
-- hook installed after this point can't silently intercept our pcall/loadstring/
-- HttpGet -- and guards.lua can compare the live globals against `env.pinned` to
-- prove nothing was swapped between capture and use.
--
-- Returns the env table directly (not a function-of-env), since it IS the root.
return (function()
  local _G_ = (getfenv and getfenv(0)) or _G or {}
  local _rawget = rawget or function(t, k) return t[k] end

  -- Pull a global through rawget first (bypasses __index lies), then fall back.
  local function grab(name, fallback)
    local ok, v = pcall(function()
      local r = _rawget(_G_, name)
      if r ~= nil then return r end
      return _G_[name]
    end)
    if ok and v ~= nil then return v end
    return fallback
  end

  local env = {}
  env.G = _G_
  env.rawget = _rawget

  -- -- core builtins (captured, immutable references) --------------------------
  env.pcall     = grab("pcall", pcall)
  env.xpcall    = grab("xpcall", xpcall)
  env.type      = grab("type", type)
  env.tostring  = grab("tostring", tostring)
  env.tonumber  = grab("tonumber", tonumber)
  env.select    = grab("select", select)
  env.error     = grab("error", error)
  env.assert    = grab("assert", assert)
  env.setmetatable = grab("setmetatable", setmetatable)
  env.getmetatable = grab("getmetatable", getmetatable)
  env.rawequal  = grab("rawequal", rawequal)
  env.rawset    = grab("rawset", rawset)
  env.next      = grab("next", next)
  env.unpack    = grab("unpack") or grab("table") and (grab("table").unpack)
  env.loadstring = grab("loadstring") or grab("load")
  env.load      = grab("load") or grab("loadstring")

  -- -- namespaces --------------------------------------------------------------
  env.string = grab("string", string)
  env.table  = grab("table", table)
  env.math   = grab("math", math)
  env.os     = grab("os", os)
  env.debug  = grab("debug", debug)
  env.coroutine = grab("coroutine", coroutine)
  env.task   = grab("task")

  -- -- Roblox surface ----------------------------------------------------------
  env.game      = grab("game")
  env.workspace = grab("workspace")
  env.typeof    = grab("typeof")
  env.Instance  = grab("Instance")
  env.Vector3   = grab("Vector3")

  -- -- executor surface (all optional) -----------------------------------------
  env.getgenv         = grab("getgenv")
  env.getrawmetatable = grab("getrawmetatable")
  env.setrawmetatable = grab("setrawmetatable")
  env.hookfunction    = grab("hookfunction")
  env.hookmetamethod  = grab("hookmetamethod")
  env.iscclosure      = grab("iscclosure")
  env.islclosure      = grab("islclosure")
  env.checkcaller     = grab("checkcaller")
  env.getcallingscript= grab("getcallingscript")
  env.getgc           = grab("getgc")
  env.getreg          = grab("getreg")
  env.getnamecallmethod = grab("getnamecallmethod")
  env.identifyexecutor  = grab("identifyexecutor") or grab("getexecutorname")
  env.request         = grab("request") or grab("http_request")
  env.syn             = grab("syn")
  env.http            = grab("http")
  env.fluxus          = grab("fluxus")
  env.gethwid         = grab("gethwid")
  env.readfile        = grab("readfile")
  env.writefile       = grab("writefile")
  env.makefolder      = grab("makefolder")
  env.HttpGet         = nil -- filled below from game

  -- -- method snapshots for swap detection -------------------------------------
  -- Capture the identity of the methods a dumper most wants to detour so guards
  -- can prove they weren't replaced between now and the handshake.
  env.snap = {}
  env.pcall(function()
    if env.game then
      env.snap.HttpGet    = env.game.HttpGet
      env.snap.GetService = env.game.GetService
    end
    if env.Instance then env.snap.InstanceNew = env.Instance.new end
  end)

  -- `pinned` = {globalName = capturedFn} that guards.checkClosureIdentity re-reads
  -- and compares by identity. Only include stable builtins that legitimately never
  -- change during a run.
  env.pinned = {}
  for _, name in ipairs({
    "pcall", "xpcall", "loadstring", "load", "select", "rawget", "rawequal",
    "setmetatable", "getmetatable", "next", "error", "assert", "tostring", "tonumber",
  }) do
    local v = env[name]
    if v ~= nil then env.pinned[name] = v end
  end

  -- -- cooperative yielder ------------------------------------------------------
  -- Heavy pure-Lua work (crypto over the delivered blob, guard scans) would lock
  -- a frame if run straight through -- that is the "game freezes on auto-execute"
  -- symptom. env.yield() hands control back to the scheduler, but only once ~6ms
  -- of busy work has accumulated, so throughput stays high while the frame never
  -- stalls. No-op when task.wait is missing or we are not in a yieldable thread
  -- (pcall'd), so it is always safe to call.
  do
    local _clock = (env.os and env.os.clock) or (os and os.clock)
    local _wait  = env.task and env.task.wait
    local _last  = _clock and _clock() or 0
    env.yield = function()
      if not _wait or not _clock then return end
      local now = _clock()
      if now - _last > 0.006 then
        pcall(_wait)
        _last = _clock()
      end
    end
  end

  return env
end)()

end)()
  local crypto = (function()
-- SolAuth protected-build crypto core (pure Lua / Luau-safe).
-- Deliberately dependency-free: SHA-256, HMAC-SHA256, base64, and an
-- HMAC-CTR stream cipher with an encrypt-then-MAC integrity tag. Every
-- primitive here has a byte-identical Node.js counterpart in
-- lib/protect/nodecrypto.js so the server can encrypt what the client decrypts.
--
-- All arithmetic is kept in the FLOAT domain (values reduced mod 2^32 as
-- doubles) so it is correct on Luau (numbers are doubles) and also on any Lua
-- with a 32-bit integer subtype. Bit ops go through a local `bit` table; on
-- Luau `bit32` always exists, and the test harness injects a float-safe shim.
return function(bit, yield)
  bit = bit or bit32
  local _y = yield or function() end  -- cooperative yielder (no-op if absent)
  local MOD = 2.0 ^ 32
  local band, bor, bxor, bnot = bit.band, bit.bor, bit.bxor, bit.bnot
  local rshift, lshift = bit.rshift, bit.lshift
  local rrotate = bit.rrotate or function(x, n)
    return (bor(rshift(x, n), lshift(x, 32 - n))) % MOD
  end

  local byte, char, sub, rep = string.byte, string.char, string.sub, string.rep
  local concat = table.concat
  local floor = math.floor

  -- -- SHA-256 ----------------------------------------------------------------
  local K = {
    0x428a2f98,0x71374491,0xb5c0fbcf,0xe9b5dba5,0x3956c25b,0x59f111f1,0x923f82a4,0xab1c5ed5,
    0xd807aa98,0x12835b01,0x243185be,0x550c7dc3,0x72be5d74,0x80deb1fe,0x9bdc06a7,0xc19bf174,
    0xe49b69c1,0xefbe4786,0x0fc19dc6,0x240ca1cc,0x2de92c6f,0x4a7484aa,0x5cb0a9dc,0x76f988da,
    0x983e5152,0xa831c66d,0xb00327c8,0xbf597fc7,0xc6e00bf3,0xd5a79147,0x06ca6351,0x14292967,
    0x27b70a85,0x2e1b2138,0x4d2c6dfc,0x53380d13,0x650a7354,0x766a0abb,0x81c2c92e,0x92722c85,
    0xa2bfe8a1,0xa81a664b,0xc24b8b70,0xc76c51a3,0xd192e819,0xd6990624,0xf40e3585,0x106aa070,
    0x19a4c116,0x1e376c08,0x2748774c,0x34b0bcb5,0x391c0cb3,0x4ed8aa4a,0x5b9cca4f,0x682e6ff3,
    0x748f82ee,0x78a5636f,0x84c87814,0x8cc70208,0x90befffa,0xa4506ceb,0xbef9a3f7,0xc67178f2,
  }

  local function preprocess(msg)
    local len = #msg
    local bitlen = len * 8
    msg = msg .. "\128"
    while (#msg % 64) ~= 56 do msg = msg .. "\0" end
    -- 64-bit big-endian length. Messages here never exceed 2^32 bits, so the
    -- top 4 bytes are always zero and only the low 32 bits carry information.
    local low = bitlen
    for i = 7, 0, -1 do
      if i < 4 then
        msg = msg .. char(floor(low / (2 ^ (i * 8))) % 256)
      else
        msg = msg .. "\0"
      end
    end
    return msg
  end

  local function sha256_bin(msg)
    msg = preprocess(msg)
    local h0,h1,h2,h3 = 0x6a09e667+0.0,0xbb67ae85+0.0,0x3c6ef372+0.0,0xa54ff53a+0.0
    local h4,h5,h6,h7 = 0x510e527f+0.0,0x9b05688c+0.0,0x1f83d9ab+0.0,0x5be0cd19+0.0
    local w = {}
    local _cc = 0
    for chunk = 1, #msg, 64 do
      _cc = _cc + 1
      if _cc % 24 == 0 then _y() end  -- yield during long hashes (e.g. blob tag)
      for i = 0, 15 do
        local a,b,c,d = byte(msg, chunk + i*4, chunk + i*4 + 3)
        w[i+1] = (((a + 0.0) * 256 + b) * 256 + c) * 256 + d
      end
      for i = 17, 64 do
        local w15 = w[i-15]; local w2 = w[i-2]
        local s0 = bxor(bxor(rrotate(w15,7), rrotate(w15,18)), rshift(w15,3))
        local s1 = bxor(bxor(rrotate(w2,17), rrotate(w2,19)), rshift(w2,10))
        w[i] = (w[i-16] + s0 + w[i-7] + s1) % MOD
      end
      local a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h = h0,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6,h7
      for i = 1, 64 do
        local S1 = bxor(bxor(rrotate(e,6), rrotate(e,11)), rrotate(e,25))
        local ch = bxor(band(e,f), band((bnot(e)) % MOD, g))
        local t1 = (h + S1 + ch + K[i] + w[i]) % MOD
        local S0 = bxor(bxor(rrotate(a,2), rrotate(a,13)), rrotate(a,22))
        local maj = bxor(bxor(band(a,b), band(a,c)), band(b,c))
        local t2 = (S0 + maj) % MOD
        h=g; g=f; f=e; e=(d+t1)%MOD; d=c; c=b; b=a; a=(t1+t2)%MOD
      end
      h0=(h0+a)%MOD; h1=(h1+b)%MOD; h2=(h2+c)%MOD; h3=(h3+d)%MOD
      h4=(h4+e)%MOD; h5=(h5+f)%MOD; h6=(h6+g)%MOD; h7=(h7+h)%MOD
    end
    local function w32(x)
      return char(floor(x / 16777216) % 256, floor(x / 65536) % 256, floor(x / 256) % 256, floor(x) % 256)
    end
    return w32(h0)..w32(h1)..w32(h2)..w32(h3)..w32(h4)..w32(h5)..w32(h6)..w32(h7)
  end

  local hexchars = "0123456789abcdef"
  local function tohex(s)
    local out = {}
    for i = 1, #s do
      local b = byte(s, i)
      out[#out+1] = sub(hexchars, floor(b/16)+1, floor(b/16)+1) .. sub(hexchars, (b%16)+1, (b%16)+1)
    end
    return concat(out)
  end

  -- -- HMAC-SHA256 -------------------------------------------------------------
  local function hmac_bin(key, msg)
    if #key > 64 then key = sha256_bin(key) end
    if #key < 64 then key = key .. rep("\0", 64 - #key) end
    local o, ip = {}, {}
    for i = 1, 64 do
      local k = byte(key, i)
      o[i]  = char(bxor(k, 0x5c) % 256)
      ip[i] = char(bxor(k, 0x36) % 256)
    end
    return sha256_bin(concat(o) .. sha256_bin(concat(ip) .. msg))
  end

  -- -- Base64 ------------------------------------------------------------------
  local b64a = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
  local b64dec = {}
  for i = 1, #b64a do b64dec[byte(b64a, i)] = i - 1 end
  local function b64encode(data)
    local out = {}
    local n = #data
    for i = 1, n, 3 do
      local a = byte(data, i)
      local b = byte(data, i + 1)
      local c = byte(data, i + 2)
      local t = a * 65536 + (b or 0) * 256 + (c or 0)
      local c1 = floor(t / 262144) % 64
      local c2 = floor(t / 4096) % 64
      local c3 = floor(t / 64) % 64
      local c4 = t % 64
      out[#out+1] = sub(b64a, c1+1, c1+1) .. sub(b64a, c2+1, c2+1)
        .. (b and sub(b64a, c3+1, c3+1) or "=")
        .. (c and sub(b64a, c4+1, c4+1) or "=")
    end
    return concat(out)
  end
  local function b64decode(str)
    local out = {}
    local bits, acc = 0, 0
    for i = 1, #str do
      if i % 900 == 0 then _y() end
      local v = b64dec[byte(str, i)]
      if v then
        acc = acc * 64 + v
        bits = bits + 6
        if bits >= 8 then
          bits = bits - 8
          out[#out+1] = char(floor(acc / (2 ^ bits)) % 256)
          -- Keep ONLY the leftover (not-yet-emitted) low `bits` bits. Without
          -- this, acc grows 64x per char and overflows a Luau double to inf/NaN
          -- on large ciphertexts (e.g. the ~50KB delivered blob) -> string.char
          -- gets NaN -> "invalid argument #1 to 'char'". Bounds acc < 2^13.
          acc = acc % (2 ^ bits)
        end
      end
    end
    return concat(out)
  end

  -- -- HMAC-CTR stream cipher (encrypt-then-MAC) -------------------------------
  -- keystream block i = HMAC(key, nonce .. ":" .. i); XOR against data. The
  -- integrity tag = HMAC(key, "tag" .. nonce .. ciphertext). decrypt() returns
  -- nil on a bad tag (tamper / wrong key), never partial plaintext.
  local function ctr_keystream(key, nonce, needed)
    local blocks = {}
    local i = 0
    local have = 0
    while have < needed do
      blocks[#blocks+1] = hmac_bin(key, nonce .. ":" .. i)
      have = have + 32
      i = i + 1
      _y()  -- yield between keystream blocks (this is the hot path on big blobs)
    end
    return concat(blocks)
  end
  local function xor_bytes(data, ks)
    local out = {}
    for i = 1, #data do
      out[i] = char(bxor(byte(data, i), byte(ks, i)) % 256)
      if i % 1500 == 0 then _y() end
    end
    return concat(out)
  end
  local function encrypt(key, nonce, plaintext)
    local ks = ctr_keystream(key, nonce, #plaintext)
    local ct = xor_bytes(plaintext, ks)
    local tag = hmac_bin(key, "tag" .. nonce .. ct)
    return b64encode(ct), tohex(tag)
  end
  local function decrypt(key, nonce, ctB64, tagHex)
    local ct = b64decode(ctB64)
    local expect = tohex(hmac_bin(key, "tag" .. nonce .. ct))
    if expect ~= tagHex then return nil end
    local ks = ctr_keystream(key, nonce, #ct)
    return xor_bytes(ct, ks)
  end

  return {
    sha256 = function(s) return tohex(sha256_bin(s)) end,
    sha256_bin = sha256_bin,
    hmac = function(k, m) return tohex(hmac_bin(k, m)) end,
    hmac_bin = hmac_bin,
    b64encode = b64encode,
    b64decode = b64decode,
    encrypt = encrypt,
    decrypt = decrypt,
    tohex = tohex,
  }
end

end)()(bit, env.yield)
  local guards = (function()
-- SolAuth protected-build guard suite (pure Lua / Luau-safe).
--
-- This module is the client-side security layer that the protect engine embeds
-- around a user's script. It exposes a battery of independent checks -- hook,
-- debugger, VM/sandbox, HTTP-spy, environment, and integrity detection -- plus a
-- runner that executes them and reports every detection through a caller-supplied
-- sink. NOTHING here decides the punishment: the footer assembled by lib/protect.js
-- owns the kill/handshake-refuse policy. Keeping detection and reaction separate
-- means the same suite can be unit-tested (report into a table) and shipped
-- (report into a kill switch) without changing a line.
--
-- EVERY executor/Roblox primitive is reached through feature detection + pcall,
-- so the module loads and runs to completion on a stock Lua VM (e.g. the fengari
-- test harness) -- where, correctly, almost every check trips because a stock VM
-- is exactly the "not a real Roblox executor" case we want to reject.
--
-- Usage (from the assembled header):
--   local guards = <embedded guards module>(_env)
--   local report = guards.run({ expectedPlaceId = 123, sourceChecksum = "ab12...",
--                               selfSource = _protectedChunkString })
--   -- report.detections = { {id=, category=, detail=}, ... }
--   -- report.blocked     = #detections > 0
return function(env)
  env = env or {}

  -- Resolve primitives once, tolerating any of them being nil. `rawget` on the
  -- real global table dodges __index hooks that lie about what exists.
  local G = env.G or (getfenv and getfenv(0)) or _G or {}
  local function pick(name)
    local ok, v = pcall(function()
      if rawget then
        local r = rawget(G, name)
        if r ~= nil then return r end
      end
      return G[name]
    end)
    if ok then return v end
    return nil
  end

  local _pcall     = env.pcall or pcall
  local _type      = env.type or type
  local _tostring  = env.tostring or tostring
  local _select    = select
  local _rawequal  = rawequal
  local _rawget    = rawget
  local _yield     = env.yield or function() end  -- cooperative yielder (anti-freeze)

  -- Executor surface (all optional).
  local _getgenv        = env.getgenv        or pick("getgenv")
  local _getrawmetatable= env.getrawmetatable or pick("getrawmetatable")
  local _hookfunction   = env.hookfunction   or pick("hookfunction")
  local _hookmetamethod = env.hookmetamethod or pick("hookmetamethod")
  local _iscclosure     = env.iscclosure     or pick("iscclosure")
  local _islclosure     = env.islclosure     or pick("islclosure")
  local _checkcaller    = env.checkcaller    or pick("checkcaller")
  local _getcallingscript = env.getcallingscript or pick("getcallingscript")
  local _getgc          = env.getgc          or pick("getgc")
  local _getreg         = env.getreg         or pick("getreg")
  local _getnamecallmethod = env.getnamecallmethod or pick("getnamecallmethod")
  local _debug          = env.debug          or pick("debug") or debug
  local _identifyexecutor = env.identifyexecutor or pick("identifyexecutor") or pick("getexecutorname")

  -- Roblox surface (all optional).
  local _game     = env.game     or pick("game")
  local _workspace= env.workspace or pick("workspace")
  local _task     = env.task     or pick("task")
  local _string   = string
  local _tableFind = (table and table.find) or nil

  -- -- detection sink ---------------------------------------------------------
  -- Every detection carries a SEVERITY:
  --   "hard" -- unambiguous "this is not a real Roblox client in the right game,
  --            or it is being actively dumped". A legitimate user on a normal
  --            executor NEVER trips these, so it is safe for the server to refuse
  --            (return garbage) on any hard hit.
  --   "soft" -- advisory. Real executors legitimately trip these (they wrap
  --            builtins / request / the debug library / newcclosure), so they
  --            must NOT block delivery or every user of those executors breaks.
  --            They are still reported for logging/telemetry.
  -- This split is the whole reason the old loader.js AT-suite couldn't be fed to
  -- a hard gate: it flagged mere executor capability as tamper.
  local CATEGORY_SEVERITY = {
    vm = "hard", env = "hard", loader = "hard", integrity = "hard",
    hook = "soft", httpspy = "soft", debugger = "soft", runtime = "soft",
  }
  local detections = {}
  local seen = {}
  local function flag(id, category, detail, severity)
    if seen[id] then return end
    seen[id] = true
    severity = severity or CATEGORY_SEVERITY[category] or "soft"
    detections[#detections + 1] = { id = id, category = category, detail = detail or "", severity = severity }
  end

  -- Small helper: is `f` a genuine C builtin (not replaced by a Lua hook)?
  -- Returns true when we can PROVE it is native, false when we can prove it is
  -- hooked, and nil when the environment can't tell us (no iscclosure). Callers
  -- treat nil as "unknown, don't flag" to avoid false positives on executors
  -- that lack the primitive.
  local function isNative(f)
    if _type(f) ~= "function" then return false end
    if _iscclosure then
      local ok, res = _pcall(_iscclosure, f)
      if ok then return res end
    end
    if _islclosure then
      local ok, res = _pcall(_islclosure, f)
      if ok then return not res end
    end
    return nil
  end

  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- | CATEGORY: hook detection                                                   |
  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- Any of the core builtins an executor script relies on being replaced with a
  -- Lua closure is the single loudest sign of a spy/dumper wrapper. We check the
  -- functions a dumper most wants to intercept.
  local HOOK_TARGETS = {
    "loadstring", "pcall", "xpcall", "pairs", "ipairs", "next", "select",
    "rawget", "rawset", "rawequal", "setmetatable", "getmetatable",
    "tostring", "tonumber", "print", "warn", "require", "error", "assert",
    "collectgarbage", "unpack",
  }
  local function checkHookedBuiltins()
    for _, name in ipairs(HOOK_TARGETS) do
      local f = pick(name)
      if _type(f) == "function" then
        local native = isNative(f)
        if native == false then
          flag("hook:" .. name, "hook", name .. " is a Lua closure (hooked)")
        end
      end
    end
  end

  -- Executor detour APIs being present AND already having intercepted something
  -- is expected on any executor, so mere presence is not a detection. Instead we
  -- confirm our own critical closures were not detoured by comparing identity
  -- captured at load with identity now (the footer passes originals in ctx).
  local function checkClosureIdentity(ctx)
    if not ctx or not ctx.pinned then return end
    for name, original in pairs(ctx.pinned) do
      local now = pick(name)
      if original ~= nil and now ~= nil and not _rawequal(original, now) then
        flag("swap:" .. name, "hook", name .. " swapped after load")
      end
    end
  end

  -- newcclosure wrapper sniffing: many hook frameworks wrap our calls so that
  -- checkcaller lies. If checkcaller exists we assert that, called from our own
  -- thread, it reports us as the caller (true). A framework that always returns
  -- false to hide game calls will trip this.
  local function checkCheckcaller()
    if not _checkcaller then return end
    local ok, res = _pcall(_checkcaller)
    if ok and res == false then
      flag("checkcaller:false", "hook", "checkcaller() false in our own thread")
    end
  end

  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- | CATEGORY: debugger / debug-library abuse                                   |
  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- A dumper commonly leans on debug.getinfo / debug.getupvalue / debug.traceback
  -- to walk our closures. We can't remove those, but we can detect when they have
  -- been REPLACED with Lua closures (a logging shim) rather than being native.
  local function checkDebugLibrary()
    if _type(_debug) ~= "table" then
      flag("debug:missing", "debugger", "debug table absent")
      return
    end
    local watch = { "getinfo", "getupvalue", "setupvalue", "getconstant",
                    "setconstant", "getupvalues", "getproto", "getstack",
                    "setstack", "traceback" }
    for _, name in ipairs(watch) do
      local f = _debug[name]
      if _type(f) == "function" then
        local native = isNative(f)
        if native == false then
          flag("debug:" .. name, "debugger", "debug." .. name .. " hooked")
        end
      end
    end
  end

  -- If debug.getinfo works, our own running function must trace back to a
  -- non-numbered, non-file source. A debugger that rewrites source to disk or a
  -- proxied chunk changes what getinfo reports about us.
  local function checkSelfTrace()
    if _type(_debug) ~= "table" or _type(_debug.getinfo) ~= "function" then return end
    local ok, info = _pcall(_debug.getinfo, checkSelfTrace, "S")
    if ok and _type(info) == "table" then
      local src = _tostring(info.short_src or info.source or "")
      -- A traced source pointing at a real on-disk path means someone dumped us
      -- to a file and is running the copy.
      if src:find("%.lua") or src:find("[/\\]") then
        flag("debug:selfsrc", "debugger", "self source on disk: " .. src)
      end
    end
  end

  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- | CATEGORY: VM / sandbox / emulator detection                                |
  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- We must be running inside a genuine Roblox client. These structural facts are
  -- expensive to fake convincingly and cheap for us to assert.
  local function checkRobloxShape()
    if _game == nil then
      flag("vm:nogame", "vm", "game global absent")
      return
    end
    -- game must expose GetService and the core services. We probe several
    -- services a real client always has; an emulated `game` stub rarely mocks
    -- them all.
    local ok = _pcall(function()
      assert(_type(_game.GetService) == "function" or _type(_game.GetService) == "userdata")
      for _, name in ipairs({ "Players", "RunService", "HttpService", "ReplicatedStorage" }) do
        assert(_game:GetService(name) ~= nil, name)
      end
    end)
    if not ok then
      flag("vm:services", "vm", "core Roblox services unreachable")
    end
    if _workspace == nil then
      flag("vm:noworkspace", "vm", "workspace absent")
    end
    if _task == nil or _type(_task) ~= "table" then
      flag("vm:notask", "vm", "task scheduler absent")
    end
  end

  -- Dumper/reverser tools frequently plant sentinel globals to coordinate with a
  -- companion process, or leave them from a previous stripping pass. Their mere
  -- presence is a hard signal -- unlike executor APIs (hookfunction, getgc), no
  -- legitimate executor sets these. This is one of the few "presence == bad"
  -- checks that is safe to hard-block on.
  local DUMPER_SENTINELS = {
    "__DUMPED", "__reversed", "__anti_debug", "__deobf", "__decompiled",
    "__DUMPER", "__unpacked", "__spy", "__hookactive", "__logger",
  }
  local function checkDumperSentinels()
    for _, name in ipairs(DUMPER_SENTINELS) do
      local v = pick(name)
      if v ~= nil then
        flag("sentinel:" .. name, "loader", "dumper sentinel global present: " .. name)
      end
    end
  end

  -- Roblox userdata/typeof surface. On a stock Lua VM these are missing; on an
  -- emulated environment they are often wrong or throw.
  local function checkTypeofSurface()
    local typeof_ = pick("typeof")
    if _type(typeof_) ~= "function" then
      flag("vm:notypeof", "vm", "typeof absent")
      return
    end
    local ok, t = _pcall(typeof_, _game)
    if not ok or (t ~= "Instance" and _game ~= nil) then
      flag("vm:typeof", "vm", "typeof(game) unexpected: " .. _tostring(t))
    end
    local okv, tv = _pcall(function()
      local Vector3 = pick("Vector3")
      return typeof_(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
    end)
    if not okv or tv ~= "Vector3" then
      flag("vm:vector3", "vm", "Vector3 datatype broken")
    end
  end

  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- | CATEGORY: HTTP spy / network interception                                  |
  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- The delivery/handshake traffic is the crown jewel. If any request primitive
  -- has been swapped for a Lua closure, a proxy is logging our calls. Check the
  -- whole family executors expose.
  local function checkHttpSpy()
    local names = { "request", "http_request", "syn_request" }
    for _, name in ipairs(names) do
      local f = pick(name)
      if _type(f) == "function" then
        if isNative(f) == false then
          flag("httpspy:" .. name, "httpspy", name .. " hooked")
        end
      end
    end
    -- syn.request / http.request nested tables.
    for _, holder in ipairs({ "syn", "http" }) do
      local t = pick(holder)
      if _type(t) == "table" and _type(t.request) == "function" then
        if isNative(t.request) == false then
          flag("httpspy:" .. holder .. ".request", "httpspy", holder .. ".request hooked")
        end
      end
    end
    -- HttpService:RequestAsync / HttpGet via namecall is the other spy point.
    if _game then
      local ok, hs = _pcall(function() return _game:GetService("HttpService") end)
      if ok and hs then
        local okm = _pcall(function()
          local m = hs.RequestAsync
          if _type(m) == "function" and isNative(m) == false then
            flag("httpspy:RequestAsync", "httpspy", "HttpService.RequestAsync hooked")
          end
        end)
        local _ = okm
      end
    end
  end

  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- | CATEGORY: environment validation                                           |
  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- Right game. The protect engine bakes the allowed PlaceId(s) into ctx.
  local function checkPlace(ctx)
    if not ctx or ctx.expectedPlaceId == nil then return end
    if _game == nil then return end -- already flagged by vm checks
    local ok, pid = _pcall(function() return _game.PlaceId end)
    if not ok then
      flag("env:placeread", "env", "PlaceId unreadable")
      return
    end
    local allowed = ctx.expectedPlaceId
    if _type(allowed) ~= "table" then allowed = { allowed } end
    local match = false
    for _, a in ipairs(allowed) do
      if _tostring(a) == _tostring(pid) then match = true break end
    end
    if not match then
      flag("env:place", "env", "PlaceId " .. _tostring(pid) .. " not authorized")
    end
  end

  -- A real executor identity string. Not a hard block on its own (names vary),
  -- but a totally empty identity on an environment that claims executor APIs is
  -- suspicious.
  local function checkExecutorIdentity()
    if not _identifyexecutor then return end
    local ok, name = _pcall(_identifyexecutor)
    if not ok or _type(name) ~= "string" or #name == 0 then
      -- Soft: some legitimate executors return an empty identity string.
      flag("env:noexec", "env", "identifyexecutor returned nothing", "soft")
    end
  end

  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- | CATEGORY: runtime / memory integrity                                       |
  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- The engine passes a checksum of the protected chunk's own text. If the loader
  -- handed us a patched body (bytes edited to strip checks), the checksum differs.
  local function checkSelfChecksum(ctx)
    if not ctx or not ctx.selfSource or not ctx.sourceChecksum or not ctx.hash then
      return
    end
    local ok, actual = _pcall(ctx.hash, ctx.selfSource)
    if not ok then
      flag("integrity:hashfail", "integrity", "self-hash threw")
      return
    end
    if _tostring(actual) ~= _tostring(ctx.sourceChecksum) then
      flag("integrity:checksum", "integrity", "self checksum mismatch")
    end
  end

  -- getgc scan: a dumper often parks a big string of our decrypted source in the
  -- registry/gc. We can't see the future, but we CAN assert our own decrypt key
  -- material is not already floating as a loose string when we start (it would be
  -- if a previous hooked run leaked it). ctx.secretMarkers holds short unique
  -- sentinels the engine planted; finding them in gc before we place them means a
  -- replay/leak.
  local function checkGcLeak(ctx)
    if not _getgc or not ctx or not ctx.secretMarkers then return end
    local ok, gc = _pcall(_getgc, false)
    if not ok or _type(gc) ~= "table" then return end
    local markers = ctx.secretMarkers
    -- Bound the scan so a huge live heap can't turn this into a multi-second
    -- stall, and yield periodically so whatever we do scan never locks a frame.
    local n = #gc
    if n > 60000 then n = 60000 end
    for i = 1, n do
      if i % 2000 == 0 then _yield() end
      local v = gc[i]
      if _type(v) == "string" and #v < 512 then
        for _, m in ipairs(markers) do
          if v ~= m and _string.find(v, m, 1, true) then
            flag("integrity:gcleak", "integrity", "secret marker already in gc")
            return
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- | CATEGORY: loader / anti-patch integrity                                    |
  -- +--------------------------------------------------------------------------+
  -- Our footer sets a private sentinel in a captured local the moment the header
  -- finishes. If the body was cut (e.g. header ran, checks NOP'd, footer spliced),
  -- the sentinel handshake token is wrong. ctx.sentinel is compared by the footer;
  -- here we assert the environment did not pre-seed a fake one in getgenv.
  local function checkGenvPollution(ctx)
    if not _getgenv or not ctx or not ctx.genvGuardKey then return end
    local ok, gv = _pcall(_getgenv)
    if not ok or _type(gv) ~= "table" then return end
    local existing = _rawget and _rawget(gv, ctx.genvGuardKey) or gv[ctx.genvGuardKey]
    if existing ~= nil then
      flag("loader:genvpollution", "loader", "guard key already set in getgenv (re-run/inject)")
    end
  end

  -- -- runner ------------------------------------------------------------------
  local function run(ctx)
    ctx = ctx or {}
    -- Each check is pcall-isolated: one throwing must not stop the rest, and a
    -- check that throws is itself a soft signal (environment fighting us).
    local checks = {
      { "hookedBuiltins", checkHookedBuiltins },
      { "closureIdentity", function() checkClosureIdentity(ctx) end },
      { "checkcaller",     checkCheckcaller },
      { "debugLibrary",    checkDebugLibrary },
      { "selfTrace",       checkSelfTrace },
      { "robloxShape",     checkRobloxShape },
      { "dumperSentinels", checkDumperSentinels },
      { "typeofSurface",   checkTypeofSurface },
      { "httpSpy",         checkHttpSpy },
      { "place",           function() checkPlace(ctx) end },
      { "executorIdentity",checkExecutorIdentity },
      { "selfChecksum",    function() checkSelfChecksum(ctx) end },
      { "gcLeak",          function() checkGcLeak(ctx) end },
      { "genvPollution",   function() checkGenvPollution(ctx) end },
    }
    for _, entry in ipairs(checks) do
      local ok, err = _pcall(entry[2])
      if not ok then
        flag("threw:" .. entry[1], "runtime", _tostring(err))
      end
    end
    -- Split by severity. Only HARD detections gate delivery; soft ones are
    -- advisory (executors legitimately trip them).
    local hard, soft = {}, {}
    for _, d in ipairs(detections) do
      if d.severity == "hard" then hard[#hard + 1] = d else soft[#soft + 1] = d end
    end
    return {
      detections = detections,
      hard = hard,
      soft = soft,
      blocked = #hard > 0,   -- NOTE: only hard signals block
      count = #detections,
      hardCount = #hard,
      softCount = #soft,
    }
  end

  return {
    run = run,
    -- Exposed individually so the test harness and the footer can call one at a
    -- time and so future checks can be composed without re-running everything.
    isNative = isNative,
    _flag = flag,
    _detections = detections,
  }
end

end)()(env)
  local footer = (function()
-- SolAuth protected-build footer / watchdog (pure Lua / Luau-safe).
--
-- Receives the outcome of the header's handshake and enforces policy:
--   * fail-closed: if the handshake returned no runnable function (bad key,
--     wrong HWID, wrong game, killed build, tamper-refusal, or any transport
--     failure), kick the player with an opaque message and raise -- the user's
--     source was never decrypted, so nothing leaks,
--   * run the recovered user script under pcall,
--   * start a background kill-switch watchdog that re-polls the server; if the
--     build is revoked mid-session the player is kicked,
--   * scrub the sensitive references it can reach after starting the script.
--
-- ctx = { f, err, env, crypto, cfg }
return function(ctx)
  local env    = ctx.env
  local cfg    = ctx.cfg
  local pcall    = env.pcall or pcall
  local type     = env.type or type
  local tostring = env.tostring or tostring
  local game     = env.game
  local task     = env.task

  local function kick(msg)
    pcall(function()
      if game then
        local Players = game:GetService("Players")
        if Players and Players.LocalPlayer then
          Players.LocalPlayer:Kick(tostring(msg))
        end
      end
    end)
    error(tostring(msg), 0)
  end

  -- -- fail-closed gate --------------------------------------------------------
  local f = ctx.f
  if type(f) ~= "function" then
    -- Deliberately vague: never tell an attacker which check failed.
    kick(ctx.err or "[SolAuth] Authorization failed")
    return
  end

  -- -- kill-switch watchdog ----------------------------------------------------
  -- Polls /builds/:id/alive on an interval. A revoked build (admin PATCH kill)
  -- flips the response; we kick on the next poll. Uses the same request-family /
  -- HttpGet fallback the handshake used. Runs in the background so it never
  -- blocks the user's script.
  local function pickRequest()
    local r = env.request
      or (env.syn and env.syn.request)
      or env.http_request
      or (env.http and env.http.request)
      or (env.fluxus and env.fluxus.request)
    if type(r) == "function" then return r end
    return nil
  end
  local function httpGet(url)
    local req = pickRequest()
    if req then
      local ok, res = pcall(req, { Url = url, Method = "GET" })
      if ok and type(res) == "table" then
        local body = res.Body or res.body
        if body then return tostring(body) end
      end
    end
    if game then
      local ok, body = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, url, true)
      if ok and type(body) == "string" then return body end
    end
    return nil
  end

  local function startWatchdog()
    if not task or type(task.spawn) ~= "function" then return end
    local url = cfg.baseUrl .. "/builds/" .. cfg.buildId .. "/alive"
    task.spawn(function()
      -- small initial jitter so a whole server's clients don't poll in lockstep
      local ok, wait = pcall(function() return task.wait end)
      if not ok or type(wait) ~= "function" then return end
      task.wait(20 + (math.random and math.random() * 10 or 0))
      while true do
        local body = httpGet(url)
        if body and (body:find('"killed"%s*:%s*true') or body:find("KILLED", 1, true)) then
          kick("[SolAuth] This build has been revoked.")
          return
        end
        task.wait(45)
      end
    end)
  end
  pcall(startWatchdog)

  -- -- run the user's script ---------------------------------------------------
  -- Scrub what we can still reach. kInner and the decrypted source were locals
  -- inside handshake.lua and are already unreachable; f does not capture header
  -- locals (it is a fresh chunk). We drop our own handles to crypto/config here.
  ctx.crypto = nil
  ctx.err = nil

  local ok, e = pcall(f)
  if not ok then
    -- The user's own script errored -- surface it, but keep our prefix so support
    -- can tell protection errors from script errors apart.
    error("[SolAuth] script runtime error: " .. tostring(e), 0)
  end
end

end)()

  local cfg = {
    baseUrl = "https://solauth.online",
    buildId = "dcadb725e8401ff3a9e52fc0",
    placeIds = nil,
    genvGuardKey = "_sz_1d9b7a0aa661",
  }

  -- Run the guard suite. Only HARD detections (non-Roblox VM / wrong game /
  -- dumper sentinels) block; soft signals (hooked request/loadstring on normal
  -- executors) never block a legit user. env.yield keeps it off the frame.
  local blocked = false
  do
    local ok, rep = (env.pcall or pcall)(function()
      return guards.run({
        expectedPlaceId = cfg.placeIds,
        hash = function(s) return crypto.sha256(s) end,

        genvGuardKey = cfg.genvGuardKey,
      })
    end)
    if ok and rep and rep.blocked then blocked = true end
  end

  local f

    local src = table.concat({"if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end\n\n-- LPH macro fallbacks (no-ops when not running under Luraph obfuscation)\nif LPH_OBFUSCATED == nil then\n    local 16.850713   -- standalone UPPER platform layer\nlocal COLUMN_SPLIT_X = -410       -- between the west (idx 1-4) and east (idx 5-8) base columns\nlocal FRONT_Z_CLAMP  = 18         -- front slides toward the player within +/-this of the base center (off the corner pillars)\nlocal SIDE_NEAR_Z    = 45         -- a side platform belongs to a base if within this Z of its center (own ~25, neighbor ~80)\n\n-- Pet's base index 1..8: planar (XZ) nearest base center, like the 400KB reference.\nlocal function getClosestBaseIdx(pos)\n    local closest, dist = 1, math.huge\n    for i = 1, 8 do\n        local b = BASES_LOW[i]\n        local d = (pos.X - b.X)^2 + (pos.Z - b.Z)^2\n        if d < dist then dist = d; closest = i end\n    end\n    return closest\nend\n\n-- FRONT (center of the front face) staging coord + (Vector3) facing for a base.\nlocal function buildFrontCandidate(idx, isUpper, playerZ)\n    local base = isUpper and BASES_HIGH[idx] or BASES_LOW[idx]\n    local frontY = isUpper and FRONT_Y_HIGH or FRONT_Y_LOW\n    local frontZ = math.clamp(playerZ - base.Z, -FRONT_Z_CLAMP, FRONT_Z_CLAMP) + base.Z\n    local coord = Vector3.new(base.X, frontY, frontZ)\n    local faceDir = (idx <= 4) and Vector3.new(-1, 0, 0) or Vector3.new(1, 0, 0)\n    return coord, faceDir\nend\n\nlocal function plotSides(coordTable, idx)\n    local base = BASES_LOW[idx]\n    local isWest = <= 9 then\n                solid = true\n                break\n            end\n        end\n    end)\n    return solid\nend\n\nlocal function isPlotUnlocked(plotName)\n    local ok, res = pcall(function()\n        local channel = getPlotChannel(plotName)\n        if not channel then return false end\n        if channelGet(channel, \"BlockEndTimeFirstFloor\") ~= nil then return false end\n        -- channel says open -- require the physical laser to agree before flying in\n        return not _floor1LaserSolid(plotName)\n    end)\n    return ok and (res == true)\nend\n\nlocal function findClosest(petPos, coordTable)\n    local best, bestKey, bestDist = nil, nil, math.huge\n    for skyKey, coords in pairs(coordTable) do\n        for _, data"," in ipairs(coords) do\n            local c = data.coord\n            local d = math.sqrt((petPos.X - c.X)^2 + (petPos.Z - c.Z)^2)\n            if d < bestDist then bestDist = d; best = data; bestKey = skyKey end\n        end\n    end\n    return best, bestKey\nend\n\n-- =====================================================================\n-- Path visualization helpers\n-- =====================================================================\nlocal _vizParts = {}\nlocal function clearViz()\n    for _, p in ipairs(_vizParts) do if p and p.Parent then p:Destroy() end end\n    table.clear(_vizParts)\nend\nlocal function vizLine(a, b, color)\n    local d = b - a\n    if d.Magnitude < 0.05 then return end\n    local p = Instance.new(\"Part\")\n    p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanQuery = false; p.CastShadow = false\n    p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; p.Color = color\n    p.Size = Vector3.new(0.4, 0.4, d.Magnitude)\n    p.CFrame = CFrame.new((a + b) / 2, b)\n    p.Parent = workspace\n    _vizParts[#_vizParts + 1] = p\nend\nlocal function vizDot(pos, color, sz)\n    local p = Instance.new(\"Part\")\n    p.Anchored = true; p.CanCollide = false; p.CanQuery = false; p.CastShadow = false\n    p.Shape = Enum.PartType.Ball; p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; p.Color = color\n    p.Size = Vector3.new(sz, sz, sz)\n    p.Position = pos\n    p.Parent = workspace\n    _vizParts[#_vizParts + 1] = p\nend\nlocal function vizPath(fromPos, waypoints)\n    if #waypoints == 0 then return end\n    vizDot(fromPos, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1.8)\n    local prev = fromPos\n    for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do\n        vizLine(prev, wp, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120))\n        vizDot(wp, Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0), 1.6)\n        prev = wp\n    end\nend\n\n-- =====================================================================\n-- Velocity mover\n-- =====================================================================\nlocal SPEED = 125\nlocal ARRIVE = 3\nlocal _STRIP_OK = (type(getconnections) == \"function\")\nlocal function _climbCap()\n    local v = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.VanishClimb) or 200, 100, 250)\n    if not _STRIP_OK then v = 55 end\n    return v\nend\n\nlocal _tpLVAtt, _tpLV\nlocal function lvDrive(hrp, v)\n    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent then return end\n    if not (_tpLV and _tpLV.Parent and _tpLVAtt and _tpLVAtt.Parent == hrp) then\n        if _tpLV then pcall(function() _tpLV:Destroy() end) end\n        if _tpLVAtt then pcall(function() _tpLVAtt:Destroy() end) end\n        _tpLVAtt = Instance.new(\"Attachment\")\n        _tpLVAtt.Name = \"VanishTPAtt\"\n        _tpLVAtt.Parent = hrp\n        _tpLV = Instance.new(\"LinearVelocity\")\n        _tpLV.Name = \"VanishTPLV\"\n        _tpLV.Attachment0 = _tpLVAtt\n        _tpLV.RelativeTo = Enum.ActuatorRelativeTo.World\n        pcall(function() _tpLV.ForceLimitsEnabled = false end)\n        _tpLV.MaxForce = math.huge\n        _tpLV.VectorVelocity = Vector3.zero\n        _tpLV.Parent = hrp\n    end\n    _tpLV.VectorVelocity = v\nend\nlocal function lvStop(hrp)\n    if _tpLV then pcall(function() _tpLV:Destroy() end); _tpLV = nil end\n    if _tpLVAtt then pcall(function() _tpLVAtt:Destroy() end); _tpLVAtt = nil end\n    if hrp and hrp.Parent then\n        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero\n        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero\n    end\nend\nlocal function vZero(hrp)\n    lvStop(hrp)\nend\n\nlocal function velMoveThrough(hrp, waypoints, speedOverride, allowJump, quickStart)\n    if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or #waypoints == 0 then return end\n    local _runSpeed = speedOverride or (_G.TPVelocity and math.clamp(_G.TPVelocity, 200, 500)) or CARPET_SPEED\n    local wpIdx = 1\n    local done = false\n    local conn\n    local function finish()\n        if done then return end\n        done = true\n        if hrp and hrp.Parent then\n            lvStop(hrp)\n            local _, y = hrp.CFrame:ToEulerAnglesYXZ()\n            hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(waypoints[#waypoints]) * CFrame.Angles(0, y, 0)\n        end\n        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end\n    end\n    local function cancelStop()\n        if done then return end\n        done = true\n        if hrp and hrp.Parent then\n            lvStop(hrp)\n        end\n        if conn then conn:Disconnect() end\n    end\n    local lastDist, stall = math.huge, 0\n\n    local _ = quickStart\n    do\n        local peak = hrp.Position.Y\n        for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do if wp.Y > peak then peak = wp.Y end end\n        if peak > hrp.Position.Y + 3 then\n            local hum = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Humanoid\")\n            if hum then\n                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)\n                pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end)\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\n    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()\n        if not hrp or not hrp.Parent or done then\n            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end\n            return\n        end\n        if _G.MeerkoTPCancel then cancelStop() return end\n        equipCarpet()\n        local target = waypoints[wpIdx]\n        local diff = target - hrp.Position\n        local mag = diff.Magnitude\n        local _spd = _runSpeed\n        if wpIdx < #waypoints and mag < 26 then\n            local nxt = waypoints[wpIdx + 1]\n            local b = nxt - target\n            if mag > 0.1 and b.Magnitude > 0.1 and diff.Unit:Dot(b.Unit) < 0.9 then\n                _spd = math.min(_spd, 240)\n            end\n        end\n        local _arr = math.max(ARRIVE, _spd / 60 * 1.25)\n        if mag < _arr then\n            wpIdx = wpIdx + 1\n            if wpIdx > #waypoints then finish() return end\n            lastDist, stall = math.huge, 0\n            target = waypoints[wpIdx]\n            diff = target - hrp.Position\n            mag = diff.Magnitude\n        end\n\n        if mag > lastDist - 0.05 then stall = stall + 1 else stall = 0 end\n        lastDist = mag\n        if stall >= 18 then finish() return end\n        if mag >= 0.1 then\n            local dir = diff.Unit\n            if (allowJump or diff.Y > 10) and diff.Y > 5 and wpIdx < #waypoints then\n                local hum = hrp.Parent and hrp.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Humanoid\")\n                if hum then\n                    local st = hum:GetState()\n                    if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping and st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then\n                        pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping) end)\n                        pcall(function() hum.Jump = true end)\n                    end\n                end\n            end\n            local _sp = _spd\n            local _mc = _climbCap()\n            if dir.Y > 0 and dir.Y * _sp > _mc then\n                _sp = _mc / dir.Y\n            end\n            lvDrive(hrp, Vector3.new(dir.X * _sp, dir.Y * _sp, dir.Z * _sp))\n        end\n    end)\n    local totalDist = 0\n    local prev = hrp.Position\n    for _, wp in ipairs(waypoints) do\n        totalDist = totalDist + (prev - wp).Magnitude\n        prev = wp\n    end\n    local timeout = totalDist / math.min(SPEED, _runSpeed) + 2\n    local elapsed = 0\n    while not done and elapsed < timeout do\n        task.wait(0.05)\n        elapsed = elapsed + 0.05\n        if _G.MeerkoTPCancel then break end\n    end\n    if _G.MeerkoTPCancel then cancelStop() else finish() end\n    vZero(hrp)\nend\n\n-- =====================================================================\n-- Raycast / route-pulling helpers\n-- =====================================================================\nlocal _DIRS = { Vector3.new(1,0,0), Vector3.new(-1,0,0), Vector3.new(0,0,1), Vector3.new(0,0,-1) }\nlocal _STRUCT = { [\"structure base home\"] = true, [\"Wall\"] = true, [\"Floor\"] = true, [\"Roof\"] = true }\nlocal _SKIP_NAME = { [\"DeliveryHitbox\"]=true, [\"StealHitbox\"]=true, [\"LaserHitbox\"]=true,\n    [\"AnimalTarget\"]=true, [\"Multiplier\"]=true, [\"Laser\"]=true, [\"Hitbox\"]=true,\n    [\"Spawn\"]=true, [\"MainRoot\"]=true, [\"SecondFloor\"]=true, [\"ThirdFloor\"]=true, [\"Slope\"]=true }\nlocal function _blocks(inst)\n    if not inst then return false end\n    if _SKIP_NA","ME[inst.Name] then return false end\n    if inst.CanCollide then return true end\n    if _STRUCT[inst.Name] then return true end\n    local s = inst.Size\n    if s and math.max(s.X * s.Y, s.X * s.Z, s.Y * s.Z) > 150 then return true end\n    return false\nend\nlocal function _blocksWide(inst)\n    if not inst then return false end\n    if _SKIP_NAME[inst.Name] then return false end\n    if inst.CanCollide then return true end\n    if _STRUCT[inst.Name] then return true end\n    local s = inst.Size\n    -- Lower area threshold than _blocks (150 -> 30) so smaller non-collidable\n    -- objects near the path count against the clearance margin.\n    if s and math.max(s.X * s.Y, s.X * s.Z, s.Y * s.Z) > 30 then return true end\n    return false\nend\nlocal function _block(origin, target, blockFn)\n    blockFn = blockFn or _blocks\n    local rp = RaycastParams.new()\n    rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude\n    rp.IgnoreWater = true\n    local skip = {}\n    for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do\n        if pl.Character then skip[#skip + 1] = pl.Character end\n    end\n    local o = origin\n    for _ = 1, 16 do\n        rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip\n        local d = target - o\n        if d.Magnitude < 0.05 then return nil end\n        local res = workspace:Raycast(o, d, rp)\n        if not res then return nil end\n        if blockFn(res.Instance) then return res end\n        skip[#skip + 1] = res.Instance\n        o = res.Position + d.Unit * 0.3\n    end\n    return nil\nend\nlocal function _clear(a, b) return _block(a, b) == nil end\nlocal function _clearDist(origin, dir, maxD)\n    local res = _block(origin, origin + dir.Unit * maxD)\n    if not res then return maxD end\n    return (res.Position - origin).Magnitude\nend\nlocal function _len(pts)\n    local s, prev = 0, pts[1]\n    for k = 2, #pts do s = s + (pts[k] - prev).Magnitude; prev = pts[k] end\n    return s\nend\nlocal function _pull(pts)\n    if #pts <= 2 then return pts end\n    local out = { pts[1] }\n    local i = 1\n    while i < #pts do\n        local j = #pts\n        while j > i + 1 and not _clear(out[#out], pts[j]) do j = j - 1 end\n        out[#out + 1] = pts[j]\n        i = j\n    end\n    return out\nend\nlocal function _stages(toPos)\n    local st = {}\n    for _, dr in ipairs(_DIRS) do\n        local cd = _clearDist(toPos, dr, 46)\n        if cd >= 12 then st[#st + 1] = toPos + dr * math.min(cd - 5, 38) end\n    end\n    return st\nend\nlocal function _routeClear(pts)\n    for i = 1, #pts - 1 do\n        if not _clear(pts[i], pts[i + 1]) then return false end\n    end\n    return true\nend\nlocal function _peakY(pts)\n    local m = -math.huge\n    for _, p in ipairs(pts) do if p.Y > m then m = p.Y end end\n    return m\nend\nlocal function _starts(fromPos)\n    local pts = { fromPos }\n    if _block(fromPos, fromPos + Vector3.new(0, 40, 0)) then\n        for _, dr in ipairs(_DIRS) do\n            local cd = _clearDist(fromPos, dr, 40)\n            if cd >= 12 then pts[#pts + 1] = fromPos + dr * math.min(cd - 5, 34) end\n        end\n    end\n    return pts\nend\n\nlocal function _candidates(sp, stage, toPos)\n    local list = {}\n    local function add(mid)\n        if mid then list[#list + 1] = { sp, mid, stage, toPos }\n        else list[#list + 1] = { sp, stage, toPos } end\n    end\n    add(nil)\n    add(Vector3.new(stage.X, sp.Y, stage.Z))\n    add(Vector3.new(sp.X, stage.Y, sp.Z))\n    local dir = Vector3.new(stage.X - sp.X, 0, stage.Z - sp.Z)\n    if dir.Magnitude > 0.1 then\n        dir = dir.Unit\n        local perp = Vector3.new(-dir.Z, 0, dir.X)\n        for _, off in ipairs({ 20, -20, 40, -40 }) do\n            add(sp + perp * off)\n        end\n    end\n    return list\nend\n\nlocal PathfindingService = game:GetService(\"PathfindingService\")\nlocal _CLEARANCE = 16\nlocal function _clearWideRay(a, b)\n    return _block(a, b, _blocksWide) == nil\nend\nlocal _SWEEP_R = 4\nlocal _ENDPOINT_SLACK = 6\nlocal _canSphere = nil\nlocal function _sweepDir(a, b)\n    local rp = RaycastParams.new()\n    rp.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude\n    rp.IgnoreWater = true\n    local skip = {}\n    for _, pl in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do\n        if pl.Character then skip[#skip + 1] = pl.Character end\n    end\n    local o = a\n    for _ = 1, 24 do\n        rp.FilterDescendantsInstances = skip\n        local d = b - o\n        if d.Magnitude < 0.05 then return false end\n        local res\n        local ok = pcall(function() res = workspace:Spherecast(o, _SWEEP_R, d, rp) end)\n        if not ok then _canSphere = false; return nil end\n        if not res then return false end\n        if _blocks(res.Instance) then return true end\n        skip[#skip + 1] = res.Instance\n        local adv = (res.Distance or 0) - 0.05\n        if adv > 0 then o = o + d.Unit * math.min(adv, d.Magnitude) end\n    end\n    return true\nend\nlocal function _sweepBlocked(a, b, slackA, slackB)\n    if _canSphere == nil then\n        _canSphere = pcall(function()\n            workspace:Spherecast(Vector3.new(0, 10000, 0), 1, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0), RaycastParams.new())\n        end)\n    end\n    if not _canSphere then return nil end\n    local d = b - a\n    local len = d.Magnitude\n    if len < 0.1 then return false end\n    local u = d / len\n    local a2 = a + u * math.min(slackA or _ENDPOINT_SLACK, len * 0.4)\n    local b2 = b - u * math.min(slackB or _ENDPOINT_SLACK, len * 0.4)\n    local fwd = _sweepDir(a2, b2)\n    if fwd == nil then return nil end\n    if fwd then return true end\n    local rev = _sweepDir(b2, a2)\n    if rev == nil then return nil end\n    return rev\nend\n\nlocal function _clearWide(a, b, slackA, slackB)\n    -- Center line must be clear (normal block test).\n    if not _clear(a, b) then return false end\n    local sw = _sweepBlocked(a, b, slackA, slackB)\n    if sw ~= nil then return not sw end\n    local d = Vector3.new(b.X - a.X, 0, b.Z - a.Z)\n    if d.Magnitude < 0.1 then\n        local ox = Vector3.new(_CLEARANCE, 0, 0)\n        local oz = Vector3.new(0, 0, _CLEARANCE)\n        return _clearWideRay(a + ox, b + ox) and _clearWideRay(a - ox, b - ox)\n            and _clearWideRay(a + oz, b + oz) and _clearWideRay(a - oz, b - oz)\n    end\n    local perp = Vector3.new(-d.Z, 0, d.X).Unit * _CLEARANCE\n    local up = Vector3.new(0, _CLEARANCE, 0)\n    return _clearWideRay(a + perp, b + perp)\n        and _clearWideRay(a - perp, b - perp)\n        and _clearWideRay(a + up, b + up)\n        and _clearWideRay(a - up, b - up)\nend\n\nlocal function _pullWide(pts)\n    if #pts <= 2 then return pts end\n    local out = { pts[1] }\n    local i = 1\n    local n = #pts\n    while i < n do\n        local j = n\n        while j > i + 1 do\n            local a, b = out[#out], pts[j]\n            -- endpoint slack only where the merged segment touches the route's\n            -- true start/end; interior corners get the full-strength sweep.\n            local sA = (i == 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0\n            local sB = (j == n) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0\n            if _clearWide(a, b, sA, sB) then break end\n            j = j - 1\n        end\n        out[#out + 1] = pts[j]\n        i = j\n    end\n    return out\nend\n\nlocal function _pushOffWalls(pts)\n    if #pts <= 2 then return pts end\n    local MARGIN = 8\n    local MAX_PUSH = 12\n    local out = { pts[1] }\n    for i = 2, #pts - 1 do\n        local p = pts[i]\n        local shift = Vector3.zero\n        for _, dr in ipairs(_DIRS) do\n            local res = _block(p, p + dr * MARGIN, _blocks)\n            if res then\n                local dist = (res.Position - p).Magnitude\n                if dist < MARGIN then\n                    shift = shift - dr * (MARGIN - dist)\n                end\n            end\n        end\n        do\n            local resUp = _block(p, p + Vector3.new(0, MARGIN, 0), _blocks)\n            if resUp then\n                local dist = (resUp.Position - p).Magnitude\n                if dist < 4 then shift = shift + Vector3.new(0, -(4 - dist), 0) end\n            end\n        end\n        if shift.Magnitude > 0.1 then\n            if shift.Magnitude > MAX_PUSH then shift = shift.Unit * MAX_PUSH end\n   ","         local moved = p + shift\n            if _clear(out[#out], moved) then\n                out[#out + 1] = moved\n            else\n                out[#out + 1] = p\n            end\n        else\n            out[#out + 1] = p\n        end\n    end\n    out[#out + 1] = pts[#pts]\n    return out\nend\n\nlocal function computeRoute(fromPos, toPos, facingDir, maxLift)\n    -- maxLift caps how high the over-the-top candidates may climb; small for the\n    -- post-clone approach and tall for the main flight.\n    maxLift = maxLift or 44\n    -- Direct shot only when the whole swept volume is clear -- the old center-ray\n    -- accept let the body clip corners/props on \"clear\" lines.\n    if _clearWide(fromPos, toPos) then return { toPos } end\n    local entry = facingDir and (toPos - facingDir * 14) or toPos\n\n    -- Candidate router: build several route shapes, keep only those whose EVERY\n    -- segment passes the swept-volume test, string-pull each, take the shortest.\n    local best, bestLen = nil, math.huge\n    local function consider(pts)\n        if not pts or #pts < 2 then return end\n        local n = #pts\n        for i = 1, n - 1 do\n            local a, b = pts[i], pts[i + 1]\n            if (a - b).Magnitude > 0.5 then\n                local sA = (i == 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0\n                local sB = (i == n - 1) and _ENDPOINT_SLACK or 0\n                if not _clearWide(a, b, sA, sB) then return end\n            end\n        end\n        local pulled = _pullWide(pts)\n        local L = _len(pulled)\n        if L < bestLen then best, bestLen = pulled, L end\n    end\n\n    do\n        local baseY = math.max(fromPos.Y, entry.Y)\n        local mid = (fromPos + entry) * 0.5\n        for _, lift in ipairs({ 10, 16, 24, 34, maxLift }) do\n            if lift <= maxLift then\n                local cy = baseY + lift\n                local apexMid = Vector3.new(mid.X, cy, mid.Z)\n                local apexEntry = Vector3.new(entry.X, cy, entry.Z)\n                consider({ fromPos, apexMid, entry })\n                consider({ fromPos, apexEntry, entry })\n                consider({ fromPos, apexMid, apexEntry, entry })\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\n    -- 1b) OVER-THE-TOP right angle (straight up, cross, drop): longer than the\n    --     diagonals so it only wins when no gradual rise verifies.\n    do\n        local baseY = math.max(fromPos.Y, entry.Y)\n        for _, lift in ipairs({ 14, 22, 32, maxLift }) do\n            if lift <= maxLift then\n                local cy = baseY + lift\n                consider({ fromPos,\n                    Vector3.new(fromPos.X, cy, fromPos.Z),\n                    Vector3.new(entry.X, cy, entry.Z),\n                    entry })\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\n    -- 2) LATERAL: sidestep around the blocker at flight height, both sides,\n    --    increasing offsets (single kink, then a parallel dogleg).\n    do\n        local dirF = Vector3.new(entry.X - fromPos.X, 0, entry.Z - fromPos.Z)\n        if dirF.Magnitude > 0.1 then\n            dirF = dirF.Unit\n            local perp = Vector3.new(-dirF.Z, 0, dirF.X)\n            local midBase = (fromPos + entry) * 0.5\n            for _, off in ipairs({ 14, -14, 24, -24, 38, -38 }) do\n                consider({ fromPos, midBase + perp * off, entry })\n                consider({ fromPos, fromPos + perp * off, entry + perp * off, entry })\n            end\n        end\n    end\n\n    -- 3) NAVMESH: PathfindingService ground route floated up, as before -- but\n    --    now it competes on length and is segment-verified like the others.\n    local navRaw\n    do\n        local groundTo = Vector3.new(entry.X, fromPos.Y, entry.Z)\n        local path = PathfindingService:CreatePath({\n            AgentRadius = 16, AgentHeight = 5, AgentCanJump = true, AgentJumpHeight = 10, AgentMaxSlope = 89,\n        })\n        local FLOAT = 5\n        local nav = { fromPos }\n        local ok = pcall(function()\n            path:ComputeAsync(Vector3.new(fromPos.X, fromPos.Y, fromPos.Z), groundTo)\n        end)\n        if ok and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success then\n            local last = fromPos\n            for _, wp in ipairs(path:GetWaypoints()) do\n                if (wp.Position - last).Magnitude >= 8 then\n                    nav[#nav + 1] = wp.Position + Vector3.new(0, FLOAT, 0)\n                    last = wp.Position\n                end\n            end\n        end\n        nav[#nav + 1] = entry + Vector3.new(0, FLOAT, 0)\n        nav = _pushOffWalls(nav)\n        navRaw = nav\n        consider(nav)\n    end\n\n    local route = best\n    if not route and _clear(fromPos, toPos) then route = { toPos } end\n    if not route then route = _pullWide(navRaw) end\n    if (route[#route] - toPos).Magnitude > 0.5 then\n        route[#route + 1] = toPos\n    end\n    return route\nend\n\n-- =====================================================================\n-- Tool helpers\n-- =====================================================================\nlocal function equipTool(name)\n    local char = LP.Character\n    if not char or char:FindFirstChild(name) then return char ~= nil end\n    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild(\"Backpack\")\n    if not bp then return false end\n    local tool = bp:FindFirstChild(name)\n    if tool and tool:IsA(\"Tool\") then tool.Parent = char; return true end\n    return false\nend\n\nlocal function unequipAll()\n    local char, bp = LP.Character, LP.Backpack\n    if not char or not bp then return end\n    for _, t in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do\n        if t:IsA(\"Tool\") then t.Parent = bp end\n    end\nend\n\n-- =====================================================================\n-- Clone swap\n-- =====================================================================\nlocal function doClone()\n    -- xen's clone method: equip the Quantum Cloner, then fire the two remotes\n    -- directly (no clone-model polling, no UI-button clicking).\n    if not NetModule then pcall(loadNet) end\n    local char = LP.Character or LP.CharacterAdded:Wait()\n    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass(\"Humanoid\")\n    if not char or not hum then return false end\n\n    local cloner = (LP:FindFirstChild(\"Backpack\") and LP.Backpack:FindFirstChild(\"Quantum Cloner\"))\n                or char:FindFirstChild(\"Quantum Cloner\")\n    if not cloner then return false end\n\n    if cloner.Parent ~= char then\n        pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(cloner) end)\n        task.wait()\n    end\n\n    if not NetModule then return false end\n\n    local useOk = pcall(function() NetModule:RemoteEvent(\"UseItem\"):FireServer() end)\n    task.wait(0.05)\n    local telOk = pcall(function() NetModule:RemoteEvent(\"QuantumCloner/OnTeleport\"):FireServer() end)\n\n    return useOk and telOk\nend\n\n\n-- =====================================================================\n-- xen-style tween-to-pet (CFrame step tween at TPTravelSpeed). rootPart.CFrame.Position) + stepGoal\n        end\n        local dur = math.clamp(stepDist / speed, 0.02, 1)\n        local tw = _TweenTS",":Create(rootPart, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { CFrame = stepCF })\n        tw:Play()\n        tw.Completed:Wait()\n    end\n    pcall(function() rootPart.Anchored = prevAnchored end)\n    if rootPart and rootPart.Parent then lvStop(rootPart) end\nend\n\n-- Tween directly to the pet after the clone (xen tween-to-pet behavior).\nlocal function goToBrainrot(petPos)\n    if not petPos then return end\n    local char, hrp\n    local _t0 = os.clock()\n    repeat\n        char = LP.Character\n        hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild(\"HumanoidRootPart\")\n        if hrp then break end\n        RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()\n    until os.clock() -    local function parseGen(text)\n            if not text or text == \"\" then return 0 end\n            local s = tostring(text):gsub(\"%$\", \"\"):gsub(\"/s\", \"\"):gsub(\",\", \"\")\n            local num = s:match(\"^%s*([%d%.]+)\")\n            if not num then return 0 end\n            local n = tonumber(num) or 0\n            local suf = s:upper():match(\"([KMBT])\")\n            if suf == \"K\" then n = n * 1e3\n            elseif suf == \"M\" then n = n * 1e6\n            elseif suf == \"B\" then n = n * 1e9\n            elseif suf == \"T\" then n = n * 1e12 end\n            return n\n        end\n\n        local function resolveGen(model)\n            if not model then return 0, \"\", \"Brainrot\" end\n            local idx = model.Name\n            local mut = (model:GetAttribute(\"Mutation\")) or nil\n            local traits = nil\n            -- AnimalsData/AnimalsShared are top-level locals defined elsewhere in\n            -- this script; both are populated by loadModules() on startup.\n            if AnimalsData and AnimalsShared and AnimalsData[idx] then\n                local info = AnimalsData[idx]\n                local ok, gen = pcall(function()\n                    return AnimalsShared:GetGeneration(idx, mut, traits, nil)\n                end)\n                local display = (info and info.DisplayName) or idx\n                if ok and type(gen) == \"number\" then\n                    -- format a readable gen text like the game does ($1.5M/s)\n                    local t\n                    if NumberUtils and NumberUtils.ToString then\n                        local okN, str = pcall(function() return NumberUtils:ToString(gen) end)\n                        if okN and str then t = \"$\" .. str .. \"/s\" end\n                    end\n                    return gen, t or (\"$\" .. tostring(gen) .. \"/s\"), display\n                end\n            end\n            -- Fallback: BillboardGui parse (legacy behaviour).\n            return 0, \"\", (model and model.Name) or \"Brainrot\"\n        end\n\n        -- Legacy BillboardGui name+gen extractor; on","ly used if AnimalsData lookup\n        -- misses       -- ----- find the Steal ProximityPrompt for a plot+slot --------------\n        local function findStealPrompt(plotName, slot)\n            local plots = Workspace:FindFirstChild(\"Plots\"); if not plots then return nil end\n            local plotInst = plots:FindFirstChild(plotName); if not plotInst then return nil end\n            local podiums = plotInst:FindFirstChild(\"AnimalPodiums\"); if not podiums then return nil end\n            local podium = podiums:FindFirstChild(tostring(slot)); if not podium then return nil end\n            local base = podium:FindFirstChild(\"Base\")\n            local spawn = base and base:FindFirstChild(\"Spawn\")\n            -- prefer an ENABLED steal prompt; otherwise keep the DISABLED one\n            -- (someone else is mid-steal) so the loop keeps firing it and snipes\n            -- the moment it frees up.\n            local disabledFallback = nil\n            if spawn then\n                local attach = spawn:FindFirstChild(\"PromptAttachment\")\n                if attach then\n                    for _, p in ipairs(attach:GetChildren()) do\n                        if p:IsA(\"ProximityPrompt\") then\n                            if p.Enabled then return p end\n                            disabledFallback = disabledFallback or p\n                        end\n                    end\n                end\n                local startPos = spawn.Position\n                local nearest, minDist = nil, math.huge\n                for _, desc in ipairs(plotInst:GetDescendants()) do\n                    if desc:IsA(\"ProximityPrompt\") and desc.ActionText == \"Steal\" then\n                        local part = desc.Parent\n                        local pos\n                        if part and part:IsA(\"BasePart\") then pos = part.Position\n                        elseif part and part:IsA(\"Attachment\") and part.Parent and part.Parent:IsA(\"BasePart\") then pos = part.Parent.Position end\n                        if pos then\n                            local hd = math.sqrt((pos.X - startPos.X)^2 + (pos.Z - startPos.Z)^2)\n                            if hd < 5 and pos.Y > startPos.Y then\n                                local yd = pos.Y - startPos.Y\n                                if desc.Enabled then\n                                    if yd < minDist then minDist = yd; nearest = desc end\n                                else\n                                    disabledFallback = disabledFallback or desc\n                                end\n                            end\n                        end\n                    end\n                end\n                if nearest then return nearest end\n            end\n            for _, p in ipairs(podium:GetDescendants()) do\n                if p:IsA(\"ProximityPrompt\") then\n                    if p.Enabled then return p end\n                    disabledFallback = disabledFallback or p\n                end\n            end\n            return disabledFallback\n        end\n\n        local _getconns = getconnections or get_signal_cons\n        local function gatherCallbacks(signal)\n            local out = {}\n            if not _getconns then return out end\n            local ok, conns = pcall(_getconns, signal)\n            if ok and type(conns) == \"table\" then\n                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do\n                    local fn = c.Function or (c.Get and c:Get())\n                    if type(fn) == \"function\" then out[#out + 1] = fn end\n                end\n            end\n            return out\n        end\n        local function buildCallbacks(prompt)\n            if stealCache[prompt] then return stealCache[prompt] end\n            local d = {\n                hold = gatherCallbacks(prompt.PromptButtonHoldBegan),\n                trig = gatherCallbacks(prompt.Triggered),\n                ready = true,\n            }\n            stealCache[prompt] = d\n            return d\n        end\n\n        local function distToPos(pos)\n            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(\"HumanoidRootPart\")\n            if not hrp or not pos then return math.huge end\n            return (hrp.Position - pos).Magnitude\n        end\n\n        local function hideBar()\n            if activeTween then activeTween:Cancel(); activeTween = nil end\n            barFill.Size = UD","im2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)\n            barHolder.Visible = false\n        end\n\n        -- attemptSteal: hold the prompt, fill the bar over the hold window, and\n        -- fire the trigger (pickup) the moment we're within STEAL_RANGE.\n        local function attemptSteal(prompt, uid, animalPos, name)\n            if not prompt or not prompt.Parent then return false end\n            if currentTargetUID ~= uid then\n                if activeTween then activeTween:Cancel(); activeTween = nil end\n                barFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)\n                currentTargetUID = uid\n            end\n            local now = os.clock()\n            if lastFire[prompt] and (now - lastFire[prompt]) < 0.5 then return true end\n            lastFire[prompt] = now\n            local data = buildCallbacks(prompt)\n            if not (data and data.ready and (#data.hold > 0 or #data.trig > 0)) then return false end\n            data.ready = false\n            task.spawn(function()\n                for _, fn in ipairs(data.hold) do task.spawn(fn) end\n                barLabel.Text = \"Stealing \" .. tostring(name or \"brainrot\") .. \"...\"\n                barHolder.Visible = true\n                barFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)\n                if activeTween then activeTween:Cancel() end\n                activeTween = TweenService:Create(barFill, TweenInfo.new(STEAL_HOLD_MAX, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0) })\n                activeTween:Play()\n\n                local startT = tick()\n                while tick() - startT < STEAL_HOLD_MIN do\n                    if currentTargetUID ~= uid then hideBar(); data.ready = true; return end\n                    task.wait()\n                end\n\n                local alreadyInRange = distToPos(animalPos) <= STEAL_RANGE\n                local fired = false\n                while true do\n                    if tick() - startT > STEAL_HOLD_MAX then break end\n                    if not prompt.Parent then break end\n                    if currentTargetUID ~= uid then break end\n                    if distToPos(animalPos) <= STEAL_RANGE then\n                        if not alreadyInRange then task.wait(STEAL_ENTRY_DELAY) end\n                        for _, fn in ipairs(data.trig) do task.spawn(fn) end\n                        if #data.trig == 0 and typeof(fireproximityprompt) == \"function\" then\n                            pcall(fireproximityprompt, prompt)\n                        end\n                        fired = true\n                        break\n                    end\n                    task.wait()\n                end\n\n                if fired then\n                    if activeTween then activeTween:Cancel(); activeTween = nil end\n                    barFill.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)\n                    task.wait(0.15)\n                end\n                barFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)\n                barHolder.Visible = false\n                task.wait(0.05)\n                data.ready = true\n            end)\n            return true\n        end\n\n        -- target selection by mode (same rules Vanish uses)\n        local function pickTarget(pets)\n            -- manual override from the Steal Target panel takes priority\n            -- over Nearest/Highest/Priority, same as doVelocityTP.\n            local overrideUID = _G.MeerkoStealTargetUID\n            if overrideUID then\n                for _, p in ipairs(pets) do\n                    if (tostring(p.plot) .. \"|\" .. tostring(p.slot)) == overrideUID then\n                        return p\n                    end\n                end\n            end\n\n            local hrp = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(\"HumanoidRootPart\")\n            if Config.StealNearest then\n                local best, bestD = nil, math.huge\n                for _, p in ipairs(pets) do\n                    local d = hrp and (hrp.Position - p.position).Magnitude or math.huge\n                    if d < bestD then bestD = d; best = p end\n                end\n                return best\n            elseif Config.StealHighest then\n                local best = pets[1]\n                for _, p in ipairs(pets) do\n                    if (p.mps or 0) > (best.mps or 0) then best = p end\n                end\n                return best\n            else\n                -- priority: scanAllPets is already petOutranks-sorted\n                return pets[1]\n            end\n        end\n\n        -- the driver loop: re-arm the steal on the current target ~10x/sec\n        local _lastTick = 0\n        local _petsCacheT = 0\n        local _petsCache = {}\n        local _stealReadyAt = os.clock() + 3   -- hold off the steal scan ~3s after joining\n        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function()\n            if os.clock() < _stealReadyAt then return end\n            local active = Config.StealNearest or Config.StealHighest or Config.StealPriority or _G.MeerkoStealTargetUID ~= nil\n            if not active then\n                if barHolder.Visible then hideBar() end\n                currentTargetUID = nil\n                _G.MeerkoCurrentSteal = nil\n                return\n            end\n            if LocalPlayer:GetAttribute(\"Stealing\") == true then return end\n            local now = os.clock()\n            if now - _lastTick < 0.25 then return end\n            _lastTick = now\n\n            -- scanAllPets is moderately heavy; refresh the list ~1x/sec\n            if now - _petsCacheT > 1.0 then\n                local ok, pets = pcall(scanAllPets)\n                _petsCache = (ok and pets) or {}\n                _petsCacheT = now\n            end\n            if #_petsCache == 0 then if barHolder.Visible then hideBar() end return end\n\n            local tp = pickTarget(_petsCache)\n =================================================================\n    -- X-RAY BASES  (ported from .gg/twayveprivate): make base walls translucent via\n    -- LocalTransparencyModifier (client-side) Avoids slowing everything down when it's off.\n        local hookInstalled = false\n        local function installSpoofHook()\n            if hookInstalled then return end\n            hookInstalled = true\n            pcall(function()\n                if hookmetamethod and newcclosure then\n                    local oldIndex\n                    oldIndex = hookmetamethod(game, \"__index\", self:IsA(\"BasePart\")\n                            and key == \"LocalTransparencyModifier\"\n                            and xraySpoofed[self] ~= nil then\n                            return            return n:find(\"base\") or n:find(\"claim\") or p:find(\"base\") or p:find(\"claim\")\n        end\n\n        local function applyXray(obj)\n            if not Config.XrayBases then return
